Let's propose a modular optimizer:
`optimize_one(a0, max_time)` runs sequence of routines:
1. Normalize and initial score.
2. For each method cycle while time left:

   - spectral_fit for some iterations
   - if time left, small LBFGS LSE polish
   - if stagnation, random perturbation/restart within length.

Let's formulate `run_log_search(loga, max_time, label)`:
- Inputs: loga (centered array), n, M, phase, momentum initial zeros, best_sc (external? function can return best_loga,best_sc,state). But top-level no closure. We can have function return.

- Loop:
```
it=0; no_improve=0; temp=temp_start; eta=eta_start
while time<deadline:
   a=from_log(loga)
   A,c=conv_and_spectrum(a,M)
   sc=score_from_c(c,n)
   if sc < best_sc: best...; no_improve=0; maybe eta=min(eta*1.05, eta_max)
   else: no_improve+=1
   if no_improve > 80:
      # perturb: add gaussian to loga, or reset to best
      if sc > best_sc*1.1 or no_improve>300:
          loga = best_loga + gauss*0.2
          momentum=zeros
          no_improve=0
          temp = temp_start maybe
      else:
          temp = max(temp*0.8, 2) or eta reduce
   p=soft_weights(c,temp)
   P=rfft(p,M)
   RA=phase*np.conj(A)
   hconv=irfft(RA*P,M)
   hconv = hconv[:n]
   h = a * hconv  # log-gradient part
   h = h - h.mean()
   momentum = decay*momentum + h
   maxabs = np.max(np.abs(momentum))
   if maxabs > 0: scale = eta / maxabs
   delta = scale * momentum
   # clip delta
   np.clip(delta, -max_log_change, max_log_change, out=delta)
   loga = loga + delta
   loga -= loga.max()
   it++
   # adapt temp: increase slowly
   if it % 50 ==0: temp *= 1.02
   if it % 100 ==0: print
```

Implementation details:
```
def spectral_update_from_log(loga, cap_factor, flat_mix, power, blend, M):
   a=from_log(loga)
   A=rfft(a,M); C=A*A
   c=irfft(C,M)[:2n-1] # already computed maybe pass c/A to reuse
   c_pos=np.maximum(c,0)
   cmax=c_pos.max()
   if cmax<=0: return loga,a
   if power !=1:
      d = c_pos**power
   else d=c_pos.copy()
   s=d.sum(); d=d/s if s>0 else
   if cap_factor <1:
      # cap before power? Better after: d = min(d, cmax*cap_factor? but d normalized, cmax normalized. cap = c_pos.max()*cap_factor? If d normalized max=1? Actually c_pos max=cmax. cap = cmax*cap_factor. d = np.minimum(c_pos, cap); then if power? Let's do cap direct: d=np.minimum(c_pos, c_pos.max()*cap_factor)
   if flat_mix>0:
      flat=np.full(2n-1, 1/(2n-1))
      d = (1-flat_mix)*d/d.sum() + flat_mix*flat
   D=rfft(d, M)
   absC=np.abs(C)
   eps=max(absC)*1e-10
   R=np.ones_like(D)
   mask=absC>eps
   R[mask]=D[mask]/C[mask]
   logR=np.log(R)
   # Clip. For mask false logR=0. Use real/imag clip.
   maxlog=6.0*blend? or 8
   logR.real=np.clip(logR.real, -maxlog, maxlog)
   logR.imag=np.clip(logR.imag, -maxlog, maxlog)
   A_new = A * np.exp(0.5 * blend * logR)
   # Symmetrize? If A_new not Hermitian, irfft assumes; ok.
   a_new=irfft(A_new,M).real[:n]
   a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
   s=a_new.sum()
   if s<1e-6: return loga,a
   a_new/=s
   new_loga=log_from_vec(a_new)  # use log with eps
   return new_loga, a_new
```
But ratio D/C: If C has zeros, D/C huge; log clip. If target D zero where C zero? R=1 if mask false. But if C small and D not small, mask false would prevent needed change; use eps relative small, e.g. maxC*1e-12 to include. Could also use C_safe = C + eps*exp(i phase D) but log huge. Clip.

Because C=A*A can be zero at some frequencies if polynomial has zeros. Ratio may be small/large. Clip log to [-4,4] for safety.

Soft weights function:
```
def make_soft_weights(c, temp, uniform_frac=0.02):
   cmax=len(c).max()
   if cmax <=0: return ones/(len)
   if temp <= 1e-6: p=full(1/len)
   else:
      x=temp*(c/cmax - 1.0)
      m=x.max()
      p=np.exp(x-m)
      s=p.sum()
      if s==0: p=full(1/len)
      else p/=s
   if uniform_frac>0: p=(1-uniform)*p + uniform/len
   return p
```
Use only positive c; c may have tiny negative; set c=np.maximum(c,0).

`log_update_step(loga, n, M, phase, temp, uniform_frac, eta, decay, momentum, max_log_change, rng=None)`:
We need return loga,momentum,sc,c maybe. But computing c inside for p; we can do in function to reduce passing.
```
def log_update_step(loga, n, M, phase, temp, uniform_frac, eta, decay, momentum, max_log_change):
    a = a_from_log(loga)
    A = rfft(a,M)
    C = A*A
    c = irfft(C,M)[:2*n-1]
    c_pos=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cmax = float(c_pos.max())
    if cmax <=0 or not finite: sc=inf? 
    sc = 2.0*n*cmax  # sum 1
    p = make_weights(c_pos, temp, uniform_frac)
    hconv = partner_from_A(A, p, M, n, phase)
    h = a * hconv
    # weighted center
    h -= np.dot(h, a)
    momentum = decay*momentum + h
    maxabs = np.max(np.abs(momentum))
    if maxabs > 0:
       delta = momentum * (eta / maxabs)
    else delta=zeros
    np.clip(delta, -max_log_change, max_log_change, out=delta)
    new_loga = loga + delta
    new_loga = new_loga - np.max(new_loga)
    return new_loga, momentum, sc, a, c, cmax
```
But `momentum = decay*momentum + h` creates new array; okay. If h has NaN, handle.
We compute a and c each step. We also want score without FFT for score? cmax from c. Good.

However, if a sum normalized inside a_from_log exactly 1; cmax approx. Weighted center `np.dot(h,a)` uses a. Good.

Can optimize by not storing c full if only cmax and p; but need c for p. okay.

Momentum shape n; initialize zeros.

In `run_log_search`, we'll call step and manage.

```
def run_log_search(loga, n, M, phase, max_time, label, rng, anchor_loga=None):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max_time
    momentum=np.zeros(n)
    best_loga=loga.copy(); best_sc=log_score(loga) # need score. Use fast score.
    current_sc=best_sc

       else: no_improve +=1
       # adapt temp
       it +=1
       if it % 50 ==0:
          temp = min(temp*1.01 + 0.1, 800)
          if no_improve>150:

                if anchor_loga is not None and anchor_score < best_sc? maybe pass anchor_sc
                    base = anchor_loga if anchor_loga is not None else best_loga
                loga = base + (rng.normal(size=n)*noise if noise>0 else 0)
                loga = loga - max(loga)
                momentum*=0.5
                temp=10.0; eta=0.05
                best_sc? keep global? If reset to best, best same.
                no_improve=0
          elif no_improve>60:
             eta=max(0.02, eta*0.9)
       if current_sc > 1e9: reset to best
       if it%200==0 and time-last_print>1: print
    return best_loga, best_sc

Implementation:
```
def lse_score_grad_y(y, beta, M=None, phase=None):
    n=y.size
    if M is None: M=get_fft_len(n)
    S=float(np.sum(y))
    if S < 1e-9 or not finite: return 1e10, zeros
    A=np.fft.rfft(y,M)
    C=A*A
    c=np.fft.irfft(C,M)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <=0: return 1e10, zeros
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    scaled -= scaled.max()
    expv=np.exp(scaled)
    logsum=float(np.exp? Actually logsum = float(np.log(expv.sum())) after shift. L = cmax + logsum/beta
```
Since scaled shifted by max (0), logsum=log(sum exp). `L = cmax + np.log(sum_exp)/beta`. But cmax and logsum; if beta large, okay. If logsum too small, p zeros. Use `logsum = shift + log(sum)` but shift=0 after max. Actually if scaled = beta*(c-cmax), max scaled=0, so L = cmax + logsum/exp? logsum = log(sum exp(scaled)). Good.
Score = 2n L / S^2.
If S !=1, c scaled; L scale? c scales y^2; S^2 y^2; L scales y^2. Good.
`p = expv / sum_exp`.
`hconv = partner_from_A(A, p, M, n, phase)` (phase if not None else compute)
`g = 2.0 * hconv`
`grad = (2.0*n) * (g * S - 2.0*L) / (S*S*S)`
Return score, grad.
```
Note: `partner_from_A` uses RA from A = FFT(y), but y maybe not normalized; okay.

If beta huge, p one peak; gradient okay.

`build_inits(n, parent, rng)`:
Will return list of normalized arrays, maybe parent first. Ensure no duplicate exactly? Not.
```
inits=[]
if parent is not None:
   ip=interpolate_parent(parent,n)
   if ip is not None: inits.append(ip)
# uniform
inits.append(np.ones(n)/n)
# random lognormal
for scale in [0.25,0.6,1.2]:
   v=np.exp(rng.normal(0, scale, n)) + 1e-9
   inits.append(normalize_array(v))
# asymmetric geometric/sigmoid
x=np.arange(n)/float(n)
for s,lam,shift in [(0.2,2.0,0), (0.5,1.0,1), (0.3,5.0,0), (0.8,3.0,0)]:
   if shift==0:
      v=np.exp(-lam*np.abs(x-s))
   else:
      v=np.exp(-lam*np.abs(x-s)) + 0.1*np.exp(-lam*np.abs(x-1.0+s))
   inits.append(normalize_array(v+1e-9))
# sigmoid/asymmetric
for beta,c in [(5,0.3),(10,0.4),(20,0.5),(4,0.25),(8,0.6)]:
   v=0.3 + 0.7*np.tanh(beta*(x-c))
   inits.append(normalize_array(np.maximum(v,1e-6)+1e-9))
# fourier
for _ in range(6):
   v=np.ones(n)
   n_harm=rng.integers(2,6)
   freqs = sorted(rng.choice(np.arange(1, max(2,n//5)+1), size=min(n_harm, n//5), replace=False))
   weights=rng.normal(0,0.5,len(freqs)); phases=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,len(freqs))
   for f,w,ph in zip: v += w*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+ph)
   v=np.maximum(v,0.01); inits.append(normalize_array(v))
# sparse supports
for frac in [0.1,0.3,0.5]:
   k=max(2,int(frac*n))
   idx=rng.choice(n,k,replace=False)
   v=np.zeros(n); v[idx]=rng.lognormal(0,0.5,k)+1e-9
   inits.append(normalize_array(v))
# anti-pal
v=0.5+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*(x-0.25)); inits.append...
# damped oscillatory

   a=normalize_array(a)

`random_fourier_search` code:
```
def random_fourier_search(n, M, max_time, rng, current_best_score):
   x=np.arange(n)/n
   deadline=min(...)
   best_a=None; best_sc=current_best_score
   for trial in range(...):
      if time>deadline: break
      base=rng.uniform(0.8,1.4)
      nh=int(rng.integers(2,9))
      maxf=max(2,n//4)
      freqs=rng.choice(np.arange(1,maxf), size=nh, replace=False)
      v=base*np.ones(n)
      for f in freqs:
         amp=0.2*rng.normal(0,1)
         ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
         v += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + ph)
      v=np.maximum(v,1e-4)
      v/=v.sum()
      sc=score_from_vec_M(v,M)
      if sc<best_sc:
         best_sc=sc; best_a=v.copy()
         print
   return best_a,best_sc
```

def spectral_update_log(loga, cap_factor, flat_mix, power, blend, M, n):
   a=a_from_log(loga)
   A,rfft...; C=A*A; c=irfft...
   c_pos=np.maximum(c,0); cmax...
   d = c_pos.copy()
   if cap_factor <1.0: d = np.minimum(d, cmax*cap_factor)
   if power !=1.0: d = np.power(d, power) # if d all zeros? handle
   s=d.sum(); if s<=: d=full; else d/=s
   if flat_mix>0: flat=1/(2n-1); d = (1-flat_mix)*d + flat_mix*flat # since both sum1; ok
   D=rfft(d,M)
   absC=np.abs(C); eps=max(1e-30, float(absC.max())*1e-10)
   R=np.ones_like(D)
   small=absC<eps
   if np.any(small):
       ang=np.angle(D[small])

   R[~small] = D[~small]/C[~small]? But C modified only small. Use mask.
   logR=np.log(R)
   maxlog=4.0
   real=np.clip(logR.real,-maxlog,maxlog); imag=np.clip(logR.imag,-maxlog,maxlog)

`normalize_array`:
```
def normalize_array(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(max(v.size,1), dtype=np.float64) / max(v.size,1)
    return v / s
```
If v size 0, `np.ones(1)`? But length expected; if v size 0, return array length 0? In interpolation, n>0. Use `len(v)`. If 0, return empty; caller handles. But to avoid error, `if v.size ==0: return np.ones(0)`.

if p.size == 0: return None
if p.size == n: v = p.copy()
else:
   xo = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,p.size)
   xn = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   v = np.interp(xn, xo, p)
return normalize_array(v)
```
If p has NaN, `np.where finite`. If p size 1, interp works with single point? np.interp requires xp length? For 1, okay? It returns constant? I think xp length 1 works? To be safe:

`log_update_step`: Need `n` param to slice; `len(a)` maybe. Use n.
```
def log_update_step(loga, n, M, phase, temp, uniform_frac, eta, decay, momentum, max_log_change):
   a = a_from_log(loga)
   A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
   C = A * A
   c = np.fft.irfft(C, M)[:2*n-1]
   c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
   cmax = float(c.max())
   if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
       # no update? return current
       return loga, momentum, float('inf'), a, c, cmax
   sc = float(2.0*n*cmax)  # sum normalized to 1 by a_from_log
   p = make_weights(c, temp, uniform_frac)
   hconv = partner_from_A(A, p, M, n, phase)
   h = a * hconv
   # weighted center
   wc = float(np.dot(h, a))
   h = h - wc
   momentum *= decay
   momentum += h
   maxabs = float(np.max(np.abs(momentum)))
   if maxabs > 1e-300:
       delta = momentum * (eta / maxabs)
       np.clip(delta, -max_log_change, max_log_change, out=delta)
       new_loga = loga + delta
   else:
       new_loga = loga
   new_loga = new_loga - float(np.max(new_loga))
   return new_loga, momentum, sc, a, c, cmax
```
Note: `momentum *= decay` mutates input; okay. If user wants not mutated? It's run local. Good.
If temp small, p uniform, h maybe varying. Good.

`run_log_search` uses `time.monotonic()`.
Implementation details:
```
def run_log_search(loga, n, M, phase, max_time, global_deadline, label, rng):
    if max_time <=0: return loga, score_from_log(loga,M)
    run_deadline = min(time.monotonic()+max_time, global_deadline)
    momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    # tiny noise
    if rng is not None:
       loga = loga + rng.standard_normal(n)*0.005
       loga = loga - np.max(loga)
    best_loga = loga.copy()
    best_sc = np.inf
    current_loga = loga.copy()
    temp = 10.0; eta = 0.08; decay=0.45; maxlc=0.35; uniform_frac=0.03
    no_improve=0; it=0; last_print=-10.0
    while time.monotonic() < run_deadline:
       new_loga, momentum, sc, a, c, cmax = log_update_step(current_loga, n, M, phase, temp, uniform_frac, eta, decay, momentum, maxlc)
       current_loga = new_loga
       it +=1
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
           best_sc = sc; best_loga = current_loga.copy(); no_improve=0
           # adapt success
           eta = min(0.8, eta*1.01)
           maxlc = min(0.9, maxlc*1.01)
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve == 50:
               eta = max(0.02, eta*0.9)
           if no_improve == 150:
               temp = min(temp*1.5, 500.0) if temp < 500 else temp
       # periodic increases
       if it % 100 == 0:
           temp = min(temp * 1.02 + 0.1, 800.0)
       if no_improve > 300:
           base = best_loga
           if rng is not None:
              noise_scale = 0.1 if best_sc < 2.0 else 0.2
              loga = base + rng.standard_normal(n)*noise_scale
           else: loga=base.copy()
           loga = loga - np.max(loga)
           current_loga = loga
           momentum *= 0.3
           temp = max(5.0, temp*0.3)
           eta = max(0.05, eta*0.7)
           no_improve = 0
       if not np.isfinite(sc):
           current_loga = best_loga.copy()
           no_improve = 1
       # print
       if it % 500 ==0:
           now=time.monotonic();
           if now - last_print > 2.0:
              print(f"  [{label}] it={it} best={best_sc:.8f} temp={temp:.1f} eta={eta:.3f}")
              last_print=now
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
       best_sc = score_from_log(best_loga, M)
    return best_loga, best_sc
```
Potential issue: `run_log_search` uses `current_loga` updated every iter, but on no_improve reset, it sets current to base. Good.
If first sc inf because cmax 0 (a all floor?) but a_from_log normalized has positive, cmax>0. Good.
`sigma` noise 0.005 log change tiny; okay.
`uniform_frac` fixed 0.03. Maybe adapt? no.
This run might not take full max_time if run_deadline? yes.

Could pass `initial_score` to avoid first score? Not needed.

`random_fourier_search` signature: `def random_fourier_search(n, M, max_time, global_deadline, rng, current_best_score):`
It returns `(best_a, best_score)`. It should not use `global_deadline` indefinite. Implementation:
```
def random_fourier_search(...):
   if max_time<=0: return None,current_best_score
   x=np.arange(n)/float(n)
   deadline=min(time.monotonic()+max_time, global_deadline)
   best_a=None; best_sc=current_best_score
   maxfreq=max(3, n//4)
   for trial in range(200000):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
      base=float(rng.uniform(0.8,1.4))
      nh=int(rng.integers(2,9))
      max_harm = min(nh, maxfreq)
      freqs = rng.choice(np.arange(1,maxfreq+1), size=max_harm, replace=False)
      v=base*np.ones(n)
      for f in freqs:
         amp=float(rng.normal(0.0,0.45))
         ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2*np.pi))
         v += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + ph)
      v = np.maximum(v, 1e-5)
      s=float(v.sum())
      if s<=0: continue
      v/=s
      sc=score_from_vec_M(v,M) # but M in func; use score_from_vec(v,M)
      if sc<best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=v.copy()
          if trial%100==0: print
   return best_a,best_sc
```
Need top-level `score_from_vec_m`? We'll name `score_from_vec` takes M optional. Good.
This could be slow if score_from_vec creates M each time; pass M. Function signature `score_from_vec(a,M=None)`. In loop use `score_from_vec(v,M)`. Good.

`build_inits` might use `normalize_array`, rng. Need not score.

In propose, after random_fourier, we include its `best_a` in raw_inits if not None.

Sorting scored: define top-level `scored_sort_key(item)`. But `sorted(scored, key=scored_sort_key)`. Good.

`run_log_search` returns best loga and score. But if it takes time slice, it may not improve initial. Fine.

Now final `peak_shrink_polish` implementation top-level:
```
def peak_shrink_polish(a, max_rounds, top_k, M, global_deadline):
   a = normalize_array(a)
   n=a.size
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_from_vec(a,M)
   if max_rounds<=0: return best_a,best_sc
   reduce_factor=0.9
   no_improve=0
   for rnd in range(max_rounds):
       if time.monotonic() > global_deadline: break
       c=fast_conv(a,M)
       if c.size==0: break
       cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax <=0: break
       if top_k >= c.size: idx=np.nonzero(c>0)[0]
       else:
          part=np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
          idx=part[np.argsort(c[part])[::-1]]
       # reduce factor schedule
       red = 0.9 + 0.02*rnd? If no improve decrease.
       for k in idx:
          k=int(k)
          j_start=max(0,k-n+1); j_end=min(k,n-1)
          if j_end<j_start: continue
          seg=a[j_start:j_end+1]
          partner=a[k-j_end:k-j_start+1][::-1]
          contrib=seg*partner
          m=min(8, contrib.size)
          if m>0:
             if m == contrib.size: loc=np.arange(m)
             else: loc=np.argpartition(contrib, -m)[-m:]
             for li in loc:
                jj=j_start+int(li)
                a[jj]*=red
       a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
       s=float(a.sum())
       if s<1e-12:
          break
       a/=s
       sc=score_from_vec(a,M)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
          # maybe stronger? red = min(0.8, red-0.02)
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve>5:
             red = max(0.8, red-0.02)
          if no_improve>20: break
   return best_a,best_sc
```
`np.argpartition(c, -top_k)` with `top_k` positive. If c has zeros, okay. In inner loop, `red` variable maybe updated after loop; need define before for k. We can set `red = 0.92 - 0.01*min(rnd,5)` and if no_improve stronger. Simpler: `red = 0.9 if rnd <10 else 0.85`. Good.
This can be time but okay.

`polish_lbfgs` using a-domain:
Top-level `lse_score_grad_y(y,beta,M,phase)` as above. `lbfgs_a_fun(x,beta,M,phase)` calls. `run_lbfgs_a(a0,beta,maxiter,maxfun,M,phase)`.
`polish_lbfgs(a, max_time, global_deadline, n, M, phase)`:

Implementation:
```
def weighted_score_grad_y(y, weights, M, phase):
   n=len; S=sum; c=conv; w_num=sum(weights*c); score=2n*w_num/S^2
   hconv=partner_from_A(A, weights, M,n,phase)
   g=2*hconv
   grad=(2n)*(g*S - 2*w_num)/S^3
```
This is same as LSE with p fixed. If weights computed from peaks at high temp. We can run minimize on this for a few iterations; then recompute weights. This is direct peak minimization. It may be more effective than LSE because weights focus peaks, but score with weights still smooth. We can use as inner loop in `iterative_reweighting`:

`get_fft_len`:
```
def get_fft_len(n):
    L = 2*n - 1
    return 1 << (L - 1).bit_length() if L > 1 else 1
```
For L=2, (1).bit_length=1 ->2, good. For L=3,2.bit_length=2->4.

`get_phase`: `m = np.arange(M // 2 + 1, dtype=np.float64)`; `return np.exp(-2.0j * np.pi * m * (n - 1) / float(M))`.
For M maybe 1, okay.

`a_from_log`:
```
def a_from_log(loga):
    x = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0: return x.copy()
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 0.0)
    a = np.exp(x)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0: return np.ones(x.size)/x.size
    return a / s
```
If loga all -inf? `max` -inf; subtract -inf => nan. But loga finite from log_from_vec; bottom -50. If running many clips, finite. If somehow inf, handle:
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): x=np.where(finite, x, -50)
```

`log_from_vec`:
```
def log_from_vec(a, floor=1e-18):
    v=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),v,0.0)
    v=np.maximum(v, floor)
    x=np.log(v)
    x=x - float(np.max(x))
    return x
```
If v size 0, handle.

else:
  ratio=c/cmax; x=temp*(ratio-1.0); x-=x.max(); p=np.exp(x); s=p.sum(); if s<=0 p=full; else p/=s
if uniform_frac > 0: p = p*(1-uniform_frac)+uniform_frac*unif
return p
```

Let's write build_inits:
```
def build_inits(n, parent, rng):
    inits=[]
    if parent is not None:
       ip=interpolate_parent(parent,n)
       if ip is not None: inits.append(ip)
    inits.append(np.ones(n)/float(n))
    x=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)/float(n)
    scales=[0.25,0.6,1.0]
    for sc in scales:
       v=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sc, n))
       inits.append(normalize_array(v))
    # exponentials
    for s,lam in [(0.2,1.5),(0.5,1.0),(0.3,3.0),(0.8,2.0),(0.1,0.8)]:
       v=np.exp(-lam*np.abs(x-s)) + 1e-8
       inits.append(normalize_array(v))
    # sigmoids
    for beta,c in [(4,0.3),(8,0.35),(15,0.45),(3,0.25),(6,0.5),(12,0.6)]:
       v=0.3 + 0.7*np.tanh(beta*(x-c))
       inits.append(normalize_array(v))
    # fourier
    for _ in range(8):
       v=0.8+0.2*np.ones(n)
       nh=int(rng.integers(2,7))
       maxf=max(2,n//10)
       freqs=rng.choice(np.arange(1,maxf+1), size=min(nh,maxf), replace=False)
       for f in freqs:
          amp=float(rng.normal(0.0,0.5))
          ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
          v += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+ph)
       v=np.maximum(v,0.01)
       inits.append(normalize_array(v))
    # sparse
    for frac in [0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7]:
       k=max(2,int(frac*n))
       idx=rng.choice(n,k,replace=False)
       v=np.zeros(n)
       v[idx]=rng.lognormal(0.0,0.5,k)+1e-8
       inits.append(normalize_array(v))
    # damped/cos
    v=np.exp(-1.5*x)*(1+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*5*x)) + 0.1
    inits.append(normalize_array(v))
    v=np.exp(-1.5*(1-x))*(1+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*3*x*(1-2*x))) +0.1? maybe simple.
    inits.append(normalize_array(v))
    return inits
```
Could be many (~5+3+5+6+8+4+2=33). Too many initial score FFTs? 33*2 transforms=66, trivial. But sorting and top 10. Fine. Time for build_inits generating arrays: rng lognormal for n=16384 few, okay.
Maybe `v=np.exp(-1.5*(1-x))*(1+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*3*x*(1-2*x))) +0.1`. term `x*(1-2x)` okay.
Return list; if any normalize empty? no.

def run_lbfgs_weighted(a0, weights, maxfun, M, phase):
    n=a0.size
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n
    options={'maxiter': max(50,maxfun//5), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':50}
    res=minimize(lbfgs_weighted_fun, a0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(weights,M,phase), options=options)
    out=np.clip(np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64),0.0,1000.0)
    s=out.sum(); if s<1e-6: out=a0.copy()
    return out
```

`fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a, M=None):
    n=a.size
    if M is None: M=get_fft_len(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:2*n-1]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)
```
For log_update, need A. We'll duplicate `A = np.fft.rfft(a,M)`, `c=irfft(A*A,M)[:]`.

In spectral, when computing c_new for score, we use `np.fft.irfft(A_new*A_new, M)[:2*n-1]`. Good.

Now, in `lse_score_grad_y`, after `A=np.fft.rfft(y,M)`, `c=irfft(A*A,M)`. y normalized sum1 from lbfgs. If y sum not 1, still okay. We pass normalized. But if y scaled, S variable used. Good.

`lse_score_grad_y` code:
```
def lse_score_grad_y(y, beta, M, phase):
    n = y.size
    S = float(np.sum(y))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(y, M)
    C = A * A
    c = np.fft.irfft(C, M)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    scaled -= float(scaled.max())
    expv = np.exp(scaled)
    sumexp = float(expv.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sumexp) or sumexp <= 0.0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
    L = cmax + float(np.log(sumexp)) / beta
    score = 2.0 * n * L / (S * S)
    p = expv / sumexp
    hconv = partner_from_A(A, p, M, n, phase)
    g = 2.0 * hconv
    grad = (2.0 * n) * (g * S - 2.0 * L) / (S * S * S)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n)
    return float(score), grad
```
If beta is very large and c-cmax differences have floating negative beyond -inf? beta*diff maybe -1e6, exp underflow okay. `scaled.max()` should be 0. If due rounded c values > cmax? cmax max, diff<=0. Good.

`weighted_score_grad_y` similar:
```
w_num=float(np.dot(weights,c)); if <=0...
hconv=partner_from_A(A, weights,...)
g=2*hconv
grad=(2n)*(g*S - 2*w_num)/(S^3)
```
But weights p may sum not 1; if sum=1. Score =2n*w_num/S^2. Good.

`lbfgs_lse_fun`:
```
def lbfgs_lse_fun(x, beta, M, phase):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    s=float(np.sum(x))
    if not finite or s<1e-12: return 1e10, zeros
    y=x/s
    sc,g = lse_score_grad_y(y,beta,M,phase)
    if not finite: return 1e10, zeros
    g = g/s
    if not all finite: g=zeros
    return sc,g
```
Similarly weighted with weights arg.

`run_lbfgs_lse`:
```
def run_lbfgs_lse(a0, beta, maxfun, M, phase):
    n=a0.size
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n
    options={'maxiter':max(50,maxfun//5),'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':50}
    try:
       res=minimize(lbfgs_lse_fun, a0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, args=(beta,M,phase), options=options)
       out=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       out=a0.copy()
    out=np.clip(out,0.0,1000.0)
    s=out.sum()
    if not finite or s<1e-12: out=a0.copy()
    return out
```
`run_lbfgs_weighted` analogous.
Potential performance: creating bounds list each call. okay.
Could use method='L-BFGS-B' no `tol`? options okay.

def iterative_reweighting(a, max_time, global_deadline, n, M, phase):
   a=normalize_array(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_from_vec(a,M)
   if max_time <=0: return best_a,best_sc
   deadline=min(time.monotonic()+max_time, global_deadline)
   if n <= 8000: maxfun=250
   elif n <= 16000: maxfun=120
   else: maxfun=70
   outer=0; temp=150.0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
       current_a=a.copy()
       c=fast_conv(current_a,M)
       cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax<=0: break
       weights=make_weights(c, temp, 0.03)
       weights=weights/weights.sum()? make normalized.
       opt=run_lbfgs_weighted(current_a, weights, maxfun, M, phase)
       sc=score_from_vec(opt,M)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=opt; a=opt; no=0
       else:
          a=best_a.copy()
       outer +=1
       temp = min(temp*1.2+5.0, 3000.0)
       if outer%5==0: print
   return best_a,best_sc
```

`polish_lbfgs`:
```
def polish_lbfgs(a, max_time, global_deadline, n, M, phase):
   a=normalize_array(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_from_vec(a,M)
   if max_time<=0: return best_a,best_sc
   deadline=min(time.monotonic()+max_time, global_deadline)
   if n <= 8000: schedule=[(100,500),(300,350),(1000,250),(3000,150)]
   elif n <=16000: schedule=[(100,250),(300,180),(1000,120),(3000,80)]
   else: schedule=[(100,150),(300,100),(1000,70),(3000,50)]
   for beta,maxfun in schedule:
       if time.monotonic() + (100 if n>12000 else 50) > deadline: break
       opt=run_lbfgs_lse(best_a, beta, maxfun, M, phase)
       sc=score_from_vec(opt,M)
       if finite and sc<best_sc: update
   return best_a,best_sc
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing

    score(a) = 2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2

The optimizer is a hybrid of:
  - log-domain peak-reducing multiplicative updates,
  - Fourier square-root (spectral) projection toward flatter convolution
    targets,
  - peak-weighted L-BFGS subproblems,
  - LSE-smoothed L-BFGS polishing,
  - deterministic peak-shrinking local refinement.

It keeps several candidate lengths (mainly an extended length around 16384,
plus the provided parent length if available) and returns the best one found
before the time limit.
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic helpers
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def get_fft_len(n: int) -> int:
    """Next power of two >= 2n - 1, suitable for linear convolution."""
    L = 2 * int(n) - 1
    if L <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()


def get_phase(n: int, M: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Phase factor for converting FFT(a) to FFT(reversed(a)):
        FFT(rev(a))[m] = exp(-2j*pi*m*(n-1)/M) * conj(FFT(a)[m])
    """
    m = np.arange(M // 2 + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.exp(-2.0j * np.pi * m * (n - 1) / float(M))


def normalize_array(v):
    """Clip non-finite/negative values and normalize to sum 1."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(v.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(v.size)
    return v / s


def interpolate_parent(parent, n: int):
    """Linearly resample an old sequence to length n and normalize."""
    p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if p.size == 1:
        v = np.full(n, float(p[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    elif p.size == n:
        v = p.copy()
    else:
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        v = np.interp(xn, xo, p)
    return normalize_array(v)


def fast_conv(a, M: int = None) -> np.ndarray:
    """Linear self-convolution via FFT, returned non-negative."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if M is None:
        M = get_fft_len(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[: 2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def score_from_vec(a, M: int = None) -> float:
    """Exact score for a vector, using FFT for convolution."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    v = normalize_array(a)
    n = v.size
    if M is None:
        M = get_fft_len(n)
    c = fast_conv(v, M)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    cm = float(c.max())
    s = float(v.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * cm / (s * s))


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Log-domain multiplicative peak reducer
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def log_from_vec(a, floor: float = 1e-18) -> np.ndarray:
    """Centered log representation of a nonnegative vector."""
    v = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.maximum(v, floor)
    x = np.log(v)
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    return x


def a_from_log(loga: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Convert centered logs to a normalized nonnegative vector."""
    x = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, -50.0)
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 0.0)
    a = np.exp(x)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
    return a / s


def score_from_log(loga: np.ndarray, M: int) -> float:
    return score_from_vec(a_from_log(loga), M)


def make_weights(c: np.ndarray, temp: float, uniform_frac: float) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Soft weights emphasizing peaks of c.

    Uses softmax with temperature over c / max(c) - 1.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)

    if temp <= 1e-8:
        p = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        x = float(temp) * (c / cm - 1.0)
        x -= float(x.max())
        p = np.exp(x)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            p = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p = p / s

    uf = float(uniform_frac)
    if uf > 0.0:
        p = p * (1.0 - uf) + (uf / float(L))
    return p


def partner_from_A(A: np.ndarray, p: np.ndarray, M: int, n: int,
                   phase: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Return h_i = sum_j p_{i+j} y_j where FFT(y) is A.
    """
    if phase is None:
        phase = get_phase(n, M)
    RA = phase * np.conj(A)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    h = np.fft.irfft(RA * P, M)[:n]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(h, 0.0)


def log_update_step(loga, n: int, M: int, phase,
                    temp: float, uniform_frac: float,
                    eta: float, decay: float,
                    momentum: np.ndarray,
                    max_log_change: float):
    """
    One log-domain multiplicative update.

    Computes the current exact score and the weighted partner vector from the
    current peak-weighted p, then updates log(a) by a centered log-gradient.
    """
    a = a_from_log(loga)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    C = A * A
    c = np.fft.irfft(C, M)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())

    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return loga, momentum, float("inf"), a, c, cmax

    sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax)   # a is normalized to sum 1

    p = make_weights(c, temp, uniform_frac)
    hconv = partner_from_A(A, p, M, n, phase)
    h = a * hconv
    h -= float(np.dot(h, a))     # weighted mean centering
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)

    momentum *= float(decay)
    momentum += h
    momentum = np.where(np.isfinite(momentum), momentum, 0.0)

    maxabs = float(np.max(np.abs(momentum)))
    if maxabs > 1e-300:
        delta = momentum * (float(eta) / maxabs)
        np.clip(delta, -max_log_change, max_log_change, out=delta)
        new_loga = loga + delta
    else:
        new_loga = loga

    new_loga = np.where(np.isfinite(new_loga), new_loga, loga)
    new_loga = new_loga - float(np.max(new_loga))
    return new_loga, momentum, sc, a, c, cmax


def run_log_search(loga, n: int, M: int, phase,
                   max_time: float, global_deadline: float,
                   label: str, rng) -> tuple:
    """Run log-domain peak search for a time slice."""
    loga = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_loga = loga.copy()
    best_sc = float("inf")

    run_deadline = min(time.monotonic() + max_time, global_deadline)
    if run_deadline <= time.monotonic():
        best_sc = score_from_log(best_loga, M)
        return best_loga, best_sc

    momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if rng is not None:
        loga = loga + rng.standard_normal(n) * 0.003
        loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))

    temp = 10.0
    eta = 0.08
    decay = 0.45
    maxlc = 0.35
    uniform_frac = 0.03
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = 0.0

    while time.monotonic() < run_deadline:
        new_loga, momentum, sc, _, _, _ = log_update_step(
            loga, n, M, phase, temp, uniform_frac, eta, decay,
            momentum, maxlc
        )
        loga = new_loga
        it += 1

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_loga = loga.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            eta = min(0.8, eta * 1.012)
            maxlc = min(0.9, maxlc * 1.012)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve == 60:
                eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.9)
            if no_improve == 160:
                temp = min(500.0, temp * 1.4 + 5.0)

        if it % 80 == 0:
            temp = min(temp * 1.02 + 0.1, 800.0)

        if no_improve > 260:
            base = best_loga
            if rng is not None:
                noise = 0.08 if best_sc < 2.0 else 0.18
                loga = base + rng.standard_normal(n) * noise
            else:
                loga = base.copy()
            loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
            momentum *= 0.3
            temp = max(5.0, temp * 0.35)
            eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.7)
            no_improve = 0

        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            loga = best_loga.copy()
            no_improve = 1

        if it % 700 == 0:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now - last_print > 2.0:
                print(f"  log[{label}] it={it} best={best_sc:.8f} temp={temp:8.1f} eta={eta:.3f}")
                last_print = now

    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = score_from_log(best_loga, M)
    return best_loga, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Initializations and cheap random Fourier search
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def random_fourier_search(n: int, M: int, max_time: float,
                          global_deadline: float, rng,
                          current_best_score: float) -> tuple:
    """Cheap random Fourier cosine initializer search."""
    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return None, current_best_score

    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    deadline = min(time.monotonic() + max_time, global_deadline)
    best_a = None
    best_sc = current_best_score
    maxfreq = max(3, n // 4)

    for trial in range(1000000):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        base = float(rng.uniform(0.8, 1.4))
        nh = int(rng.integers(2, 9))
        nh = min(nh, maxfreq)
        freqs = rng.choice(np.arange(1, maxfreq + 1), size=nh, replace=False)
        v = base * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for f in freqs:
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.45))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            v += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * x + ph)

        v = np.maximum(v, 1e-5)
        s = float(v.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            continue
        v /= s

        sc = score_from_vec(v, M)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = v.copy()
            if trial % 200 == 0:
                print(f"  rfourier trial={trial} best={best_sc:.8f}")

    return best_a, best_sc


def build_inits(n: int, parent, rng) -> list:
    """Construct a diverse set of normalized initializations."""
    inits = []
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    if parent is not None:
        ip = interpolate_parent(parent, n)
        if ip is not None:
            inits.append(ip)

    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    # lognormal random
    for sc in (0.25, 0.6, 1.1):
        v = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sc, n)) + 1e-8
        inits.append(normalize_array(v))

    # asymmetric exponentials
    for s, lam in ((0.2, 1.2), (0.5, 1.0), (0.32, 2.8), (0.78, 2.2), (0.12, 0.9), (0.9, 1.5)):
        v = np.exp(-lam * np.abs(x - s)) + 1e-8
        inits.append(normalize_array(v))

    # sigmoid-like asymmetric steps
    for beta, c in ((4.0, 0.30), (8.0, 0.35), (15.0, 0.45), (3.0, 0.25), (6.0, 0.50), (12.0, 0.60)):
        v = 0.3 + 0.7 * np.tanh(beta * (x - c))
        inits.append(normalize_array(v))

    # random low/mid Fourier sums
    maxf = max(2, n // 10)
    for _ in range(8):
        v = 0.8 + 0.2 * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        nh = int(rng.integers(2, 7))
        nh = min(nh, maxf)
        freqs = rng.choice(np.arange(1, maxf + 1), size=nh, replace=False)
        for f in freqs:
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.55))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            v += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * x + ph)
        v = np.maximum(v, 0.01)
        inits.append(normalize_array(v))

    # sparse support randoms
    for frac in (0.10, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70):
        k = max(2, int(frac * n))
        idx = rng.choice(n, k, replace=False)
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v[idx] = rng.lognormal(0.0, 0.5, k) + 1e-8
        inits.append(normalize_array(v))

    # damped oscillatory non-symmetric trials
    v = np.exp(-1.4 * x) * (1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 5.0 * x)) + 0.1
    inits.append(normalize_array(v))
    v = np.exp(-1.4 * (1.0 - x)) * (1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * (x * (1.0 - 2.0 * x) * 4.0))) + 0.1
    inits.append(normalize_array(v))

    return inits


def scored_sort_key(item):
    return item[0]


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Spectral square-root / root-projection
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def spectral_update_log(loga, cap_factor: float, flat_mix: float,
                        power: float, blend: float, M: int, n: int):
    """
    One Fourier projection toward a target square P^2 ~= target.

    Uses A_new = A * exp(0.5 * blend * log(D/C)), where C=FFT(current conv)
    and D=FFT(target).  Returns (loga_new, score) or (None, None).
    """
    a = a_from_log(loga)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    C = A * A
    c = np.fft.irfft(C, M)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return None, None

    d = c.copy()

    if cap_factor < 1.0:
        d = np.minimum(d, cmax * float(cap_factor))

    if power != 1.0:
        d = np.power(d, float(power))

    s = float(d.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        d = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        d = d / s

    fm = float(flat_mix)
    if fm > 0.0:
        flat = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        d = (1.0 - fm) * d + fm * flat

    D = np.fft.rfft(d, M)
    absC = np.abs(C)
    eps = max(1e-300, float(absC.max()) * 1e-10)
    C_safe = C.copy()
    small = absC < eps
    if np.any(small):
        C_safe[small] = eps * np.exp(1j * np.angle(D[small]))

    R = D / C_safe
    zeroD = np.abs(D) < eps
    if np.any(zeroD):
        R[zeroD] = 1.0
    R = np.where(np.isfinite(R), R, 1.0)

    logR = np.log(R)
    lr = np.clip(logR.real, -4.0, 4.0)
    li = np.clip(logR.imag, -4.0, 4.0)
    logR = lr + 1j * li

    A_new = A * np.exp(0.5 * float(blend) * logR)
    a_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new, M).real[:n]
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
    s = float(a_new.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-8:
        return None, None

    a_new /= s
    c_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new * A_new, M)[: 2 * n - 1]
    sc = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(np.maximum(c_new, 0.0))))
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return None, None

    return log_from_vec(a_new), sc


def spectral_polish(loga, max_time: float, global_deadline: float,
                    M: int, n: int) -> tuple:
    """A safe local spectral polishing routine."""
    loga = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_loga = loga.copy()
    best_sc = score_from_log(best_loga, M)

    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return best_loga, best_sc

    deadline = min(time.monotonic() + max_time, global_deadline)
    current_loga = loga.copy()
    current_sc = best_sc

    attempts = [
        # cap, flat_mix, power, blend
        (1.00, 0.00, 1.00, 0.05),
        (1.00, 0.00, 1.00, 0.15),
        (1.00, 0.12, 1.00, 0.15),
        (0.98, 0.00, 1.00, 0.15),
        (0.95, 0.00, 1.00, 0.20),
        (0.95, 0.12, 0.85, 0.25),
        (0.90, 0.00, 0.92, 0.30),
        (0.86, 0.00, 0.86, 0.40),
        (0.82, 0.08, 0.80, 0.55),
        (0.76, 0.00, 0.75, 0.75),
        (0.70, 0.00, 0.70, 1.00),
        (0.62, 0.00, 0.65, 1.00),
    ]

    idx = 0
    stalled = 0
    total = len(attempts)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if idx >= total:
            if stalled >= total:
                break
            idx = 0

        cap, flat, powr, blend = attempts[idx]
        res = spectral_update_log(current_loga, cap, flat, powr, blend, M, n)
        if res is None or res[0] is None:
            idx += 1
            stalled += 1
            if stalled > 2 * total:
                break
            continue

        new_loga, new_sc = res
        if not np.isfinite(new_sc):
            idx += 1
            stalled += 1
            continue

        # Accept a mildly improving or neutral modal move; keep best separately.
        if new_sc < current_sc * 1.002 + 1e-9:
            current_loga = new_loga
            current_sc = new_sc

        if new_sc < best_sc - 1e-9:
            best_sc = new_sc
            best_loga = new_loga.copy()
            idx = 0
            stalled = 0
        else:
            idx += 1
            stalled += 1

        if stalled > 2 * total:
            break

    return best_loga, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Smooth quadratic objectives and L-BFGS polishers
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def lse_score_grad_y(y, beta: float, M: int, phase):
    """LSE-smoothed score and gradient with respect to y (nonnegative)."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = y.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    S = float(np.sum(y))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    A = np.fft.rfft(y, M)
    C = A * A
    c = np.fft.irfft(C, M)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    scaled = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
    scaled -= float(scaled.max())
    expv = np.exp(scaled)
    sumexp = float(expv.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sumexp) or sumexp <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    L = cmax + float(np.log(sumexp)) / float(beta)
    sc = 2.0 * n * L / (S * S)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    p = expv / sumexp
    hconv = partner_from_A(A, p, M, n, phase)
    g = 2.0 * hconv
    grad = (2.0 * n) * (g * S - 2.0 * L) / (S * S * S)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(sc), grad


def lbfgs_lse_fun(x, beta: float, M: int, phase):
    """Objective/gradient for L-BFGS in level variables x >= 0."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    S = float(np.sum(x))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)

    y = x / S
    sc, gy = lse_score_grad_y(y, beta, M, phase)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)

    gx = gy / S
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        gx = np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(sc), gx


def run_lbfgs_lse(a0, beta: float, maxfun: int, M: int, phase):
    """Run bounded L-BFGS on LSE-smoothed score."""
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a0.size
    if n == 0:
        return a0

    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n
    options = {
        "maxiter": max(50, int(maxfun) // 5),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "maxls": 50,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lbfgs_lse_fun, a0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            args=(beta, M, phase),
            options=options,
        )
        out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        out = a0.copy()

    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return a0.copy()
    return out


def weighted_score_grad_y(y, weights, M: int, phase):
    """Score/gradient for weighted sum of convolution coefficients."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = y.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    S = float(np.sum(y))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    A = np.fft.rfft(y, M)
    C = A * A
    c = np.fft.irfft(C, M)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    wn = float(weights.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(wn) or wn <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    weights = weights / wn

    wnum = float(np.dot(weights, c))
    if not np.isfinite(wnum) or wnum <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    sc = 2.0 * n * wnum / (S * S)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    hconv = partner_from_A(A, weights, M, n, phase)
    g = 2.0 * hconv
    grad = (2.0 * n) * (g * S - 2.0 * wnum) / (S * S * S)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(sc), grad


def lbfgs_weighted_fun(x, weights, M: int, phase):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    S = float(np.sum(x))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)

    y = x / S
    sc, gy = weighted_score_grad_y(y, weights, M, phase)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)

    gx = gy / S
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        gx = np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(sc), gx


def run_lbfgs_weighted(a0, weights, maxfun: int, M: int, phase):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a0.size
    if n == 0:
        return a0

    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n
    options = {
        "maxiter": max(50, int(maxfun) // 5),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "maxls": 50,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lbfgs_weighted_fun, a0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            args=(weights, M, phase),
            options=options,
        )
        out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        out = a0.copy()

    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return a0.copy()
    return out


def iterative_reweighting(a, max_time: float, global_deadline: float,
                          n: int, M: int, phase) -> tuple:
    """
    Alternatively target current peaks by solving weighted quadratic
    subproblems with L-BFGS.
    """
    a = normalize_array(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_from_vec(a, M)

    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return best_a, best_sc

    deadline = min(time.monotonic() + max_time, global_deadline)
    safe_margin = 90.0 if n > 12000 else 45.0

    if n <= 8000:
        maxfun = 220
    elif n <= 16000:
        maxfun = 110
    else:
        maxfun = 65

    outer = 0
    temp = 120.0

    while time.monotonic() + safe_margin < deadline:
        c = fast_conv(a, M)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            break

        weights = make_weights(c, temp, 0.04)
        opt = run_lbfgs_weighted(a, weights, maxfun, M, phase)
        sc = score_from_vec(opt, M)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = opt.copy()
            a = opt.copy()
        else:
            a = best_a.copy()

        outer += 1
        temp = min(temp * 1.22 + 6.0, 4000.0)

        if outer % 5 == 0:
            print(f"  iter-rew outer={outer} best={best_sc:.8f}")

    return best_a, best_sc


def polish_lbfgs(a, max_time: float, global_deadline: float,
                 n: int, M: int, phase) -> tuple:
    """Polish with LSE-smoothed L-BFGS over several temperatures."""
    a = normalize_array(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_from_vec(a, M)

    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return best_a, best_sc

    deadline = min(time.monotonic() + max_time, global_deadline)
    safe = 90.0 if n > 12000 else 45.0

    if n <= 8000:
        schedule = [(100, 420), (300, 300), (1000, 220), (3000, 140)]
    elif n <= 16000:
        schedule = [(100, 240), (300, 170), (1000, 110), (3000, 70)]
    else:
        schedule = [(100, 150), (300, 100), (1000, 70), (3000, 45)]

    for beta, maxfun in schedule:
        if time.monotonic() + safe > deadline:
            break
        opt = run_lbfgs_lse(best_a, beta, maxfun, M, phase)
        sc = score_from_vec(opt, M)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = opt.copy()
            print(f"  lbfgs-lse beta={beta} best={best_sc:.8f}")

    return best_a, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Deterministic peak-shrinking local search
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def peak_shrink_polish(a, max_rounds: int, top_k: int, M: int,
                       global_deadline: float) -> tuple:
    """Directly shrink top contributing entries of active peaks."""
    a = normalize_array(a)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_from_vec(a, M)
    if max_rounds <= 0 or n == 0:
        return best_a, best_sc

    top_k = max(4, int(top_k))
    no_improve = 0

    for rnd in range(int(max_rounds)):
        if time.monotonic() > global_deadline:
            break

        c = fast_conv(a, M)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            break

        if top_k >= c.size:
            idx = np.nonzero(c > 0.0)[0]
        else:
            part = np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
            idx = part[np.argsort(c[part])[::-1]]

        red = 0.92 - 0.004 * min(rnd, 12)
        if no_improve > 5:
            red = max(0.80, red - 0.01)

        for k0 in idx:
            k = int(k0)
            j_start = max(0, k - n + 1)
            j_end = min(k, n - 1)
            if j_end < j_start:
                continue

            seg = a[j_start:j_end + 1]
            partner = a[k - j_end:k - j_start + 1][::-1]
            contrib = seg * partner
            m = min(6, contrib.size)
            if m <= 0:
                continue

            if m == contrib.size:
                loc = np.arange(contrib.size)
            else:
                loc = np.argpartition(contrib, -m)[-m:]

            for li in loc:
                jj = j_start + int(li)
                a[jj] *= float(red)

        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
            break
        a /= s

        sc = score_from_vec(a, M)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 25:
                break

    return best_a, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main search driver
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, *args, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for the coefficient sequence.  Returns a Python list of nonnegative
    floats.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(total_budget) or total_budget <= 10.0:
        total_budget = 2400.0

    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    # -------------------------
    # Read parent safely
    # -------------------------
    parent = None
    MAX_OUTPUT_N = 20000
    DEFAULT_N = 16384

    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals() and height_sequence_1 is not None:
            p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 8:
                if p.size > MAX_OUTPUT_N:
                    parent = interpolate_parent(p, MAX_OUTPUT_N)
                else:
                    parent = normalize_array(p)
            else:
                parent = normalize_array(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Total usable work time (leave margin for direct final evaluation).
    total_work = max(120.0, float(total_budget) - 260.0)
    work_deadline = t_start + total_work

    # Global best can be any length.
    best_a_global = None
    best_sc_global = float("inf")

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        try:
            sc0 = score_from_vec(parent)
            if np.isfinite(sc0):
                best_a_global = parent.copy()
                best_sc_global = sc0
        except Exception:
            pass

    # -------------------------
    # Optionally polish the original parent length first
    # -------------------------
    parent_for_interp = parent

    if parent_for_interp is not None and parent_for_interp.size <= MAX_OUTPUT_N:
        parent_opt_time = min(240.0, 0.11 * total_work)
        if parent_opt_time > 35.0 and time.monotonic() + parent_opt_time < work_deadline:
            p_len = int(parent_for_interp.size)
            pM = get_fft_len(p_len)
            pphase = get_phase(p_len, pM)
            plog = log_from_vec(parent_for_interp)
            try:
                plog2, psc = run_log_search(
                    plog, p_len, pM, pphase,
                    parent_opt_time, work_deadline, "parent", rng
                )
                if np.isfinite(psc) and psc < best_sc_global:
                    pbest = a_from_log(plog2)
                    best_a_global = pbest.copy()
                    best_sc_global = psc
                    parent_for_interp = pbest.copy()
                    print(f"  parent optimized: {best_sc_global:.8f}")
            except Exception:
                pass

    # -------------------------
    # Primary length
    # -------------------------
    if parent_for_interp is not None:
        plen = int(parent_for_interp.size)
        if plen > MAX_OUTPUT_N:
            n = MAX_OUTPUT_N
        elif plen > DEFAULT_N:
            n = plen
        else:
            n = DEFAULT_N
    else:
        n = DEFAULT_N

    n = max(512, min(int(n), MAX_OUTPUT_N))
    M = get_fft_len(n)
    phase = get_phase(n, M)
    print(f"Primary optimization length n={n}, FFT M={M}")

    # -------------------------
    # Cheap global Fourier initializer
    # -------------------------
    if total_budget > 420.0:
        rf_time = min(90.0 if parent_for_interp is not None else 220.0,
                      0.08 * total_work)
        if rf_time > 25.0:
            a_rf, sc_rf = random_fourier_search(
                n, M, rf_time, work_deadline, rng, best_sc_global
            )
            if a_rf is not None and np.isfinite(sc_rf) and sc_rf < best_sc_global:
                best_a_global = a_rf.copy()
                best_sc_global = sc_rf
                print(f"  random-fourier improved: {best_sc_global:.8f}")
            rf_best = a_rf
        else:
            rf_best = None
    else:
        rf_best = None

    # Primary-level best (must be length n).
    best_a_primary = None
    best_sc_primary = float("inf")

    # -------------------------
    # Build primary initializations
    # -------------------------
    raw_inits = build_inits(n, parent_for_interp, rng)
    if rf_best is not None:
        raw_inits.append(rf_best)

    scored = []
    for a0 in raw_inits:
        a0 = normalize_array(a0)
        if a0.size != n:
            a0 = interpolate_parent(a0, n)
            if a0 is None:
                continue
        try:
            sc = score_from_vec(a0, M)
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_sc_primary:
                best_sc_primary = sc
                best_a_primary = a0.copy()
            if sc < best_sc_global:
                best_sc_global = sc
                best_a_global = a0.copy()
        scored.append((float(sc) if np.isfinite(sc) else 1e9, a0))

    if len(scored) == 0:
        u = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        scored.append((score_from_vec(u, M), u))

    scored.sort(key=scored_sort_key)

    max_inits = 10
    weights = [6, 4, 3, 3, 2, 2, 1, 1, 1, 1]
    weights = weights[:min(max_inits, len(scored))]
    sum_w = float(sum(weights))

    # Exploration phase (log-domain search from each good initializer).
    exploration_start = time.monotonic()
    exploration_deadline = min(work_deadline, exploration_start + 0.60 * (work_deadline - exploration_start))
    primary_total_time = max(30.0, exploration_deadline - exploration_start)

    print(f"Exploration: {len(scored)} inits, budget={primary_total_time:.1f}s")

    for ii in range(min(max_inits, len(scored))):
        if time.monotonic() >= exploration_deadline:
            break

        desired = (weights[ii] / sum_w) * primary_total_time
        desired = min(desired, exploration_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if desired <= 5.0:
            break

        a0 = scored[ii][1]
        loga0 = log_from_vec(a0)
        try:
            out_log, out_sc = run_log_search(
                loga0, n, M, phase,
                desired, exploration_deadline,
                f"p{ii}", rng
            )
        except Exception:
            out_log, out_sc = loga0, score_from_vec(a0, M)

        if np.isfinite(out_sc):
            out_a = a_from_log(out_log)
            if out_sc < best_sc_primary:
                best_sc_primary = out_sc
                best_a_primary = out_a.copy()
            if out_sc < best_sc_global:
                best_sc_global = out_sc
                best_a_global = out_a.copy()
            print(f"  primary init {ii}: best {out_sc:.8f}")

    if best_a_primary is None:
        best_a_primary = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_sc_primary = score_from_vec(best_a_primary, M)

    if best_a_global is None:
        best_a_global = best_a_primary.copy()
        best_sc_global = best_sc_primary

    print(f"Before polish: primary={best_sc_primary:.8f}, global={best_sc_global:.8f}")

    # -------------------------
    # Polish primary candidate
    # -------------------------
    def update_if_better(sc, a, primary_only=False):
        nonlocal best_a_primary, best_sc_primary, best_a_global, best_sc_global
        a = normalize_array(a)
        if not np.isfinite(sc) or a.size <= 0:
            return
        if not primary_only and a.size == n:
            if sc < best_sc_primary:
                best_sc_primary = sc
                best_a_primary = a.copy()
        if sc < best_sc_global:
            best_sc_global = sc
            best_a_global = a.copy()

    # (1) Spectral polynomial-sqrt polish
    time_left = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if time_left > 120.0:
        spec_time = min(0.20 * total_work, 0.35 * time_left)
        if spec_time > 30.0:
            spec_dl = min(work_deadline, time.monotonic() + spec_time)
            if best_sc_primary < best_sc_global + 1e-7:
                loga = log_from_vec(best_a_primary)
                try:
                    loga2, sc2 = spectral_polish(loga, spec_time, spec_dl, M, n)
                    update_if_better(sc2, a_from_log(loga2), primary_only=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # (2) Iterative peak reweighting
    time_left = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if time_left > 180.0 and best_sc_primary < best_sc_global + 1e-7 or best_sc_global == float("inf"):
        ir_time = min(0.24 * total_work, 0.40 * time_left)
        if ir_time > 60.0:
            ir_dl = min(work_deadline, time.monotonic() + ir_time)
            try:
                a2, sc2 = iterative_reweighting(
                    best_a_primary, ir_time, ir_dl, n, M, phase
                )
                update_if_better(sc2, a2, primary_only=True)
                print(f"  iterative reweighting: {sc2:.8f}")
            except Exception:
                pass

    # (3) More log-domain refinement from the current best
    time_left = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if time_left > 120.0:
        lr_time = min(0.16 * total_work, 0.30 * time_left)
        if lr_time > 30.0:
            lr_dl = min(work_deadline, time.monotonic() + lr_time)
            loga = log_from_vec(best_a_primary)
            try:
                loga2, sc2 = run_log_search(
                    loga, n, M, phase, lr_time, lr_dl, "polish-log", rng
                )
                update_if_better(sc2, a_from_log(loga2), primary_only=True)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # (4) Deterministic peak shrinking
    time_left = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if time_left > 60.0:
        rounds = max(10, min(45, int(time_left * 0.25)))
        try:
            a2, sc2 = peak_shrink_polish(
                best_a_primary, rounds, 12, M,
                min(work_deadline, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, 0.5 * time_left))
            )
            update_if_better(sc2, a2, primary_only=True)
            print(f"  peak shrink: {sc2:.8f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # (5) LSE L-BFGS polishing
    time_left = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if time_left > 100.0:
        lb_time = min(0.24 * total_work, 0.55 * time_left)
        if lb_time > 40.0:
            lb_dl = min(work_deadline, time.monotonic() + lb_time)
            try:
                a2, sc2 = polish_lbfgs(
                    best_a_primary, lb_time, lb_dl, n, M, phase
                )
                update_if_better(sc2, a2, primary_only=True)
                print(f"  lbfgs polish: {sc2:.8f}")
            except Exception:
                pass

    # (6) Final quick log pass if anything remains
    time_left = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if time_left > 60.0 and best_sc_primary < best_sc_global + 1e-7:
        final_time = min(120.0, 0.5 * time_left)
        if final_time > 20.0:
            final_dl = min(work_deadline, time.monotonic() + final_time)
            loga = log_from_vec(best_a_primary)
            try:
                loga2, sc2 = run_log_search(
                    loga, n, M, phase, final_time, final_dl, "final-log", rng
                )
                update_if_better(sc2, a_from_log(loga2), primary_only=True)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # -------------------------
    # Final safe return
    # -------------------------
    return_deadline = t_start + max(120.0, float(total_budget) - 90.0)
    out_arr = best_a_global if best_a_global is not None else best_a_primary

    if out_arr is None:
        out_arr = normalize_array(np.ones(1024))

    out_arr = np.asarray(out_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(out_arr), out_arr, 0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out_arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(max(1024, int(out_arr.size)), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(out_arr.size)
    out_arr = out_arr / s
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out_list = [float(z) for z in out_arr]

    # Optionally verify with the official evaluator if time permits.
    try:
        if time.monotonic() < return_deadline:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
            if np.isfinite(val):
                print(f"Verified final score: {val:.10f}, n={len(out_list)}")
            else:
                print("Final evaluator returned non-finite; returning internal best.")
    except Exception as e:
        print("Final verification skipped/failed:", repr(e))

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print(f"Returned length={len(out_list)}, elapsed={elapsed:.1f}s/{total_budget:.0f}s")
    return out_list
```
